All rates, token volumes and success probabilities are hypothetical; no measured gains, real provider prices or Astra API availability were claimed. Sensitivity shows expensive testing can eliminate savings.
- Key distinction: existing weights plus context/components does not equal training/distillation. Offline expert use has real amortized cost. The proposed online path requires only the existing cheap pool. Training is optional later and changes weights.
- Do not claim cheap models universally equal Astra. Recipe-covered tasks may improve considerably, but novel mechanics, debugging and artistic judgment can retain a model-capability gap. Give Astra the same improved tools/components/context in comparisons; do not benchmark against a weak strawman.
- Proposed first pilot is a server-authoritative coin shop in an existing game, followed by mobile HUD and checkpoint/respawn. No pilot generation was executed or charged.

## High-value findings

1. Current plugin already supports screenshots, playtest scripts/logs, assets and rollback.
2. Source acknowledges Convex concurrency pressure; current poll sleep is 1s active / up to 5s idle after grace.
3. Result delivery has no general client retry/outbox; no local request deduplication in dispatch.
4. Stop clears project context while in-flight handlers can finish later.
5. Rollback can return completed with partial failure; native recording begin method has no located call site.
6. Legacy runTests is one smoke test, with warnings classified as failures; newer playtest is richer.
7. Portrait direct-upload buffer can fail width-only resize; media already uses R2 and upload concurrency limits.
8. Logging can change cleanup control flow on 403 or render failures when enabled.
9. Live source revision safety/context indexing require validation; no backend trace yet establishes deficient retrieval.
10. OpenRouter live table showed GPT-5.6 Luna leading monthly traffic and many other models. Do not infer per-task routing or causation from aggregate tokens.

## Reproduction caveats

Offline probes characterize local module semantics. They are not Studio integration tests, a full test suite, or evidence of incident frequency. A same-service cross-project reconnect probe has lower practical reachability because App normally constructs a new Core. Portrait issue pertains to direct-upload frames; dedicated screenshot handlers force landscape. Logging issue depends on actual logger configuration. Error-free smoke testing is a weak oracle, not proof all testing is absent.

Do not repeat broad unrelated Lemonade searches. Prioritize authenticated editor evidence and actual generation traces. Preserve original evidence, and add findings with dated source references and confidence labels.
